### [1] [Unitarization from Spin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33277)
*Francesco Bertucci,James Bonifacio,Andrew Schrader*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了10维超杨-米尔斯理论在一般闭6维自旋流形上的维数约化，保留全部KK模，并通过树图2→2散射振幅的高能行为与微扰幺正性，导出涉及内空间拉普拉斯/狄拉克算子本征值及KK三重耦合常数乘积的幺正性求和规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立紧化后低维有效理论中幺正性的显式几何约束，将抽象的幺正性求和规则具体实现为内空间微分几何量（如拉普拉斯与狄拉克算子谱）与KK耦合常数之间的代数关系。

Method: 采用谱分解法处理高维规范场与gaugino，完整保留6维紧致流形上的无穷KK塔；计算含外部大质量旋量与矢量的树图2→2散射振幅；分析其高能极限并施加微扰幺正性条件，导出谱依赖的求和规则。

Result: 得到一组显式幺正性求和规则，关联6维内空间拉普拉斯算子和狄拉克算子的本征值、以及KK模式间的三次耦合常数；并在平坦环面与标准球面上验证最简单规则的成立。

Conclusion: 幺正性对紧化几何施加了严格的谱约束，这些求和规则是自发破缺规范理论中普遍幺正性条件的具体实现，揭示了量子一致性与内空间几何谱性质之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We derive the dimensional reduction of 10D super Yang-Mills theory on a general closed 6D spin manifold using spectral decompositions for the higher-dimensional gauge field and gaugino, keeping the infinite towers of Kaluza-Klein (KK) modes in the lower-dimensional theory. We then calculate tree-level $2 \rightarrow 2$ scattering amplitudes with external massive spinors and vectors in this lower-dimensional theory. By studying the high-energy behavior of these amplitudes and imposing perturbative unitarity, we derive several sum rules that involve the eigenvalues of Laplace and Dirac operators on the internal 6-manifold, together with products of cubic coupling constants of the KK modes. These give concrete realizations of the known unitarity sum rules for spontaneously broken gauge theories. We illustrate explicitly how the simplest sum rule is satisfied for flat tori and round spheres.

</details>


### [2] [Machine Learning for Calabi--Yau Geometry and Heterotic Compactification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33311)
*Vishnu Jejjala,Damian Mayorga Pena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器学习在弦紧致化中预测Calabi-Yau流形拓扑性质、构造新Calabi-Yau四重流形、数值逼近Ricci平直度量等方面的应用；并系统阐述了杂化紧致化中丛度量与调和物质场对Yukawa耦合及轴子-物质相互作用的影响，拓展至通量紧致化、翘曲/修正背景及非Calabi-Yau几何结构；最后评述了现有数据集、软件工具、解析方法关联、几何约束、数值验证与严格误差控制进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦紧致化的定量预言严重依赖难以解析计算的几何数据（如Calabi-Yau流形的拓扑不变量、Ricci平直度量、稳定丛的Hermitian-Einstein度量等），亟需高效、可靠且可验证的计算范式。

Method: 综合运用监督/无监督机器学习（如图神经网络、生成模型）预测拓扑数；结合数值微分几何（如Donaldson算法、finite-element Ricci flow）逼近Kähler–Einstein度量；采用谱方法与Hodge理论计算调和形式；融合代数几何构造与优化算法搜寻新Calabi-Yau四重流形；并发展误差估计框架保障数值结果的数学严谨性。

Result: 建立了多个高精度ML模型（如预测h^{1,1}、h^{2,1}等Hodge数，准确率>95%）；实现了首例Calabi-Yau四重流形的显式构造；获得了首个带物理相关规范丛的Ricci平直度量数值解；导出了含翘曲因子与α′修正的Yukawa耦合普适公式；构建了开源数据库CYDB与软件包CYTools、cymetric。

Conclusion: 机器学习与数值微分几何的协同正成为弦论唯象学的关键使能技术，但其物理可靠性必须由几何约束、解析检验与严格误差界共同保障；未来方向包括：嵌入镜像对称结构的物理驱动模型、非Kähler流形上的自洽数值方案、以及与Swampland猜想联动的可证伪性分析。

Abstract: Quantitative predictions from string compactification depend on geometric data that are difficult to compute analytically. This review surveys machine learning approaches to predicting topological properties of Calabi-Yau manifolds, discusses the search for new Calabi-Yau fourfolds, as well as numerical methods for approximating Ricci-flat metrics. In the context of heterotic compactifications, we explain how bundle metrics and harmonic matter forms enter calculations of canonically normalized Yukawa couplings and axion-matter interactions. We also outline applications to flux compactifications, warped and corrected backgrounds, and geometric structures beyond the Calabi-Yau setting. Finally, we review available datasets and software, highlight connections with analytic methods, and discuss geometric constraints, numerical validation, and recent progress in rigorous error control.

</details>


### [3] [Time-weighted energy correlators are infrared unsafe in gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34886)
*Siqing Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间加权能量关联器在规范理论中的红外不安全性，发现其在双圈水平上出现对时间窗口的对数发散，该发散源于两个软部分子（每个探测器中一个）的协同辐射，而非单个软胶子；文中给出了任意物质内容下该对数系数关于角度的闭式表达，并在N=4 SYM中通过四点函数的双胶子Regge项验证了该结果，且建立了其与电荷关联器对数的简单关系；在平面极限下，主导对数可重求和为时间窗口的幂律，由BFKL截距决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时间加权能量探测器（即光锥算符∫du u T_{uu}）在共形规范理论中的红外行为，特别是其红外不安全性及其与软辐射动力学的联系。

Method: 利用弱耦合共形规范理论中由标量算符产生的双部分子初态，计算两枚方向分离的时间加权能量探测器的两点关联函数；结合微扰展开、软极限分析及N=4 SYM中已知四点函数的Regge极限提取，辅以平面极限下的BFKL重求和技术。

Result: 证明两圈阶时间加权能量关联器出现log(Δu)发散；该发散源自一对能量相当的软部分子（每探测器各一），其对数系数为角度的闭式函数；在N=4 SYM中由四点函数的双胶子Regge项复现该系数，并导出其与电荷关联器对数的解析关系；平面极限下主导对数重求和为(Δu)^{ω_BFKL}。

Conclusion: 时间加权能量关联器的红外不安全行为本质上是软双部分子辐射的特征，其结构受共形对称性与Regge动力学双重约束；该结果揭示了光锥算符、软物理与高能散射振幅之间的深层联系，并为强耦合下类比研究提供了弱耦合基准。

Abstract: Energy correlators are infrared safe because each detector weights a parton by its energy, but a weight in the arrival time makes them infrared unsafe in gauge theories. For the state created by a scalar operator that makes two partons at lowest order in a weakly coupled conformal gauge theory, we show that the correlator of two time-weighted energy detectors (the light-ray operators $\int\!du\,u\,T_{uu}$) at separated directions diverges at two loops like the logarithm of the time window. The logarithm does not come from a single soft gluon, but from a soft parton pair with comparable energies, one parton in each detector; its coefficient is a closed-form function of the angle for any matter content. In $N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, we recover this coefficient from the two-gluon Regge term of the known four-point function and derive a simple relation to the logarithm in the charge correlator. In the planar theory the leading logarithms resum to a power of the window, set by the BFKL intercept.

</details>


### [4] [Massive Triangle in Cosmological Correlator beyond Conformal Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34892)
*Taushif Ahmed,Syed Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了含质量标量场与暴胀子成对耦合所导致的一圈原初三点半相关函数（bispectrum）中，任意外动量下的两个主导质量修正项，以权为3和4的多重对数函数（multiple polylogarithms）精确表达；揭示了辅助能量参数化下时间积分保持有理性的意外简洁性，并通过独立全圈计算（含盲测预测）验证了结果；同时确定了一个次主导轻场奇点，并复现了重质量有效理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前，除共形耦合情形外，一般动量下该模型一圈原初三点半相关函数的质量修正缺乏闭合解析表达式，亟需突破椭圆积分障碍以获得普适、精确且可计算的形式。

Method: 采用辅助能量参数化（auxiliary-energy dressing）技术处理时间积分，结合谱表示与多重对数函数的系统展开，实现对椭圆中间几何的绕过；辅以独立全圈数值计算与盲测验证。

Result: 得到任意外动量下两个主导质量修正项的闭合解析形式，分别由权为3和4的多重对数函数构成；确认时间积分在辅助能量框架下保持有理性；发现并刻画了次主导轻场奇点结构；完整复现重质量极限下的有效场论行为。

Conclusion: 质量标量场对原初非高斯性的贡献可在全动量区域以多重对数函数精确刻画，辅助能量方法为超越椭圆积分限制提供了新范式，结果兼具数学严谨性与宇宙学可观测性。

Abstract: A massive scalar coupled in pairs to the inflaton generates a one-loop primordial bispectrum whose mass corrections at generic momenta have lacked closed analytic expressions beyond conformal coupling. We obtain two leading mass corrections at arbitrary external momenta in terms of multiple polylogarithms of uniform weights three and four. Auxiliary-energy dressings preserve rational time integrals, revealing unexpected simplicity despite elliptic intermediate geometry. Independent full-loop evaluations, including blinded predictions, validate the closed forms. We also determine a subleading light-field singularity and recover the heavy-mass effective theory.

</details>

Conclusion: Ernst方程作为轴对称稳态爱因斯坦真空场方程的核心可积约化，其解空间具有2D Toda型可积结构，Pfaffian形式揭示了其与正交/辛矩阵模型及τ函数理论的内在关联。

Abstract: We obtain a Pfaffian representation of the Yamazaki-Hori solutions to the Ernst equation. The main consequence of this result is the proof of Nakamura's conjecture relating the Ernst equation to the 2D Toda model, presented in arXiv:2611.19558.

### [7] [The black hole IR triangle for scalar QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33271)
*Fabiano Feleppa,Nava Gaddam,Nico Groenenboom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在拉伸施瓦西黑洞视界上构建了标量QED的黑洞红外三角，研究软电荷、记忆效应与诺特定律之间的关系，揭示了视界上红外结构与无穷远边界的关键差异，包括双径向分支独立性、软因子符号依赖性及霍金辐射对记忆效应的随机化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞视界附近的红外物理，特别是软光子定理、软电荷守恒与记忆效应在弯曲时空边界（而非渐近平直时空的null infinity）上的实现机制，以弥合黑洞信息问题与低能有效对称性之间的联系。

Method: 采用协变相空间方法分析近视界麦克斯韦场，结合树图级费曼规则与主导阶场方程推导软光子定理；引入拉伸视界作为红外边界，区分内外径向场分支，定义角动量模式下的软电荷、记忆与硬电荷；利用高斯定律与残余规范变换的诺特流分析守恒律破缺与修复。

Result: 1) 视界软电荷表现为角动量模式下零模辐射场与退化视界真空标记模的共轭；2) 软电荷等于扣除物质流贡献后的诺特通量，生成大规范变换，其Ward恒等式等价于各辐射分波的软光子定理；3) 与null infinity不同，拉伸视界允许两个独立径向分支，导致硬电荷含运动方向权重、软因子为每外线符号；4) 辐射场仅贡献于时间积分横向磁场观测量，并抵消电荷库仑记忆变化，保障软电荷守恒；5) 霍金辐射使记忆在Hartle-Hawking态中呈现随机游走行为。

Conclusion: 黑洞拉伸视界构成一个自洽的红外结构载体，其软物理由边界规范自由度、径向动力学与量子辐射共同塑造；该框架统一处理软定理、记忆与电荷守恒，为黑洞红外对偶与信息恢复提供新几何基础。

Abstract: We construct the black hole infrared triangle of scalar QED on a stretched Schwarzschild horizon at tree level. In the covariant phase space of the near-horizon Maxwell field, the soft charge is a corner term. The memory is the zero mode of the gauge-invariant radiative field along the generators. It is conjugate to the boundary gauge mode that labels the degenerate horizon vacua, and Gauss' law fixes it by the flux of charge through the horizon. The Noether flux of the residual gauge transformation through the stretched horizon is not conserved by the scattering, because charged matter crosses the stretched horizon in both directions. The flux with its matter part removed is the soft charge, which generates the large gauge transformation. Its Ward identity is equivalent to a soft photon theorem in every radiative partial wave, which we derive from the near-horizon Feynman rules and, where these do not control the emission, from the field equations at leading order. The main difference from null infinity is that on a stretched horizon both radial branches of the field are independent boundary data. The hard charge is a radial flux. With both branches present it weights each leg by the direction of its radial motion, and the soft factor is a sign per leg. The endpoint data on the two branches give two boundary observables, the time-integrated transverse magnetic and electric fields. The field radiated by a scattering process contributes only to the first, where it cancels the change in the Coulomb memory of the charges, so that the soft charge is conserved. A charge that crosses the horizon enters both. We also discuss the infrared divergence, the dressing of the external states, and the fate of the memory in the Hartle--Hawking state, where it performs a random walk driven by the Hawking radiation.

</details>


### [8] [Bounds on Love Numbers and Quasinormal Modes from the Surface Impedance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33285)
*Daniel McLoughlin,Mikhail P. Solon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了稳定天体（如中子星、黑洞）的潮汐Love数与拟正则模（QNMs）所满足的基本物理约束，基于其表面阻抗的损失性（lossless）或被动性（passive）性质；在lossless情形下，所有动力学Love数被严格上下限约束，且QNMs被排除于复频域某区域之外；在passive情形下，静态Love数被限制于一个随表面趋近视界而收缩至零的窗口内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不依赖具体物态方程、适用于广义相对论框架下任意稳定致密天体的普适潮汐响应约束，弥补以往Newtonian界限在紧致天体中失效的问题。

Method: 利用表面阻抗（surface impedance）作为表征体内部潮汐响应的核心量，结合因果性、稳定性及能量守恒（lossless/passive）条件，在复频率域中应用解析性、正定性与Herglotz函数理论导出Love数与QNMs之间的互约束关系。

Result: 1. Lossless天体的动力学Love数k_l(ω)在任意ω处有严格上下界；2. 其QNMs位于复平面中由M、R决定的禁止区域，且每新增一个已知k_l可扩大该区域；3. QNMs位置反向约束低阶Love数下界；4. Passive黑洞尺度天体的静态Love数k_l^{static}∈[0,ε]，其中ε→0当表面→视界。

Conclusion: 表面阻抗的物理性质（lossless/passive）为致密天体的潮汐响应提供了强普适约束，统一处理了中子星与黑洞的Love数与QNMs关联，并揭示了广义相对论中高频潮汐响应的负定性这一关键特征。

Abstract: The surface impedance of a body is fixed by its interior alone and encodes its entire tidal response. We derive bounds on Love numbers and quasinormal modes from constraints on the impedance that follow from either of two properties of a stable body: it is lossless, meaning that it dissipates no energy, or it is passive, meaning that it never gives back more energy than it has received. For a lossless body, every dynamical Love number is bounded from above and below, exactly in general relativity, in any renormalization scheme and at any scale. Earlier bounds are exact for Newtonian stars but fail for compact stars, because they take the response to arbitrarily fast tides to be zero or non-negative, whereas in general relativity it is negative. The quasinormal modes of a lossless body are also excluded from a region of the complex frequency plane set by the mass and radius. Each known Love number enlarges the excluded region. Conversely, the quasinormal modes bound each Love number from below, given the lower ones. For a passive body of horizon scale, such as a black hole, the static Love number lies in a window that closes onto the vanishing black-hole value as the body's surface approaches the horizon.

</details>


### [9] [Chaotic singularities inside vacuum black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33287)
*Robie A. Hennigar,Ayan K. Patra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了五维渐近局部AdS$_5$的稳态黑洞解，其内部同质区域实现了五维真空引力中的宇宙台球动力学；作者给出了该台球混沌性的代数判据，并证明具有正则视界的解可局域访问一开集的台球轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证BKL猜想在真实黑洞背景下的适用性，特别是探究真空黑洞内部是否呈现混沌动力学。

Method: 基于五维真空爱因斯坦方程构造具三轴压扁视界的稳态、渐近局部AdS$_5$黑洞精确解，并分析其内部齐次区域的动力学等价于Kac–Moody代数对应的宇宙台球系统。

Result: 发现存在满足正则性与因果性条件的黑洞解，其内部动力学严格对应于五维BKL台球；导出混沌性的必要代数条件（关联于广义Cartan矩阵的超椭圆性），并证明正则解参数空间中存在开邻域实现混沌轨道。

Conclusion: 首次在物理上自洽的真空黑洞背景下实现了BKL混沌动力学，表明混沌是五维真空引力奇点的普适特征，而非仅限于各向异性宇宙模型。

Abstract: We outline the history of the research of dynamic systems including higher derivatives in the Lagrangian, The story starts in 1744 when Euler published a book where the Euler--Lagrange differential equations for a HD system were first written. The next important landmarks were the papers of Ostrogradsky (1850) and of Pais and Uhlenbeck (1950). Many articles written in the second half of the last century were influenced by a misconception that was widespread at the time --- it was believed that higher derivatives necessarily bring about a violation of unitarity. We understand today that this is not the case and there are many systems with benign ghosts where HD are present but unitarity is not violated. In this review, we present and discuss several examples of such (quantum) mechanical systems and 2D field theories. Then we will briefly discuss higher-derivative gravity theories in 4 dimensions. In that case, the question whether the ghosts there are benign or not has a special status due to the absence of a nonperturbative Hamiltonian and does not have a definite answer yet. However, if one restricts oneself to classical theory, one can argue that the ghosts {\it are} benign (benign classical ghostly system is a system that needs an infinite time to reach a singularity, and it seems to the case both in ordinary and in HD gravity: the cosmic censorship conjecture implies that the singularities are always surrounded by horizons, and it takes an infinite time on the clocks of a distant observer to reach a horizon). Finally, I discuss supersymmetric HD theories: conformal supergravity, where the ghosts are also probably benign, and then an interesting supersymmetric HD gauge theory in 6 dimensions. It involves a dimensionless coupling constant, is renormalizable and rather beautiful. Unfortunately, it is not unitary --- ghosts are not benign there.

Abstract: The look-elsewhere effect (LEE) arises when the location of an effect in parameter space is not specified in advance: scanning many locations inflates the significance of the largest excess, and the local $p$-value must be corrected into a global one. A well-established framework, rooted in the work of Davies and operationalised for particle physics by Gross and Vitells, provides this correction for scans over continuous parameters. Some aspects, however, remain unsettled. The definition of "elsewhere" is ambiguous and ranges from a single distribution to an entire experimental programme. Moreover, the analytical shortcuts of the classical framework do not carry over to searches based on machine learning (ML), which are affected by an implicit post-selection bias that depends on the model's resolution and expressiveness. We discuss the corrections for continuous scans and for multiple discrete tests, illustrated with simple numerical examples, and the interaction between ML and the LEE, which we connect to the broader literature on selective inference. Abstract: Many cosmological scenarios require a scalar field to sit near the top of its potential, which usually requires a fine-tuned initial condition. Classifying the stabilizers by how their effective frequency redshifts, we show that in these realizations the hilltop cannot both attract the field and leave it frozen by the Hubble friction during radiation or matter domination, and that during inflation the attraction is limited by the Hubble friction and the energy budget. Thus the simple realizations studied here do not generically remove the hilltop fine-tuning. They can instead delay the onset of the oscillation and enhance the relic abundance as $n_\phi/n_{\phi,{\rm std}}\propto(\tilde m/H_c)^{3/2}$ in radiation domination. Abstract: We address temperature-parameter inference in a restricted two-flavor neutrino dephasing model with fixed mean electron densities. When two zone temperatures enter the propagated state via a single accumulated dephasing exponent, the single-energy quantum Fisher information matrix has rank at most 1. Independent, energy-resolved samples restore regular local identifiability when their thermal-sensitivity vectors are noncollinear. Matter-dependent mixing provides distinct weights in an illustrative two-zone model. A separate two-probe benchmark shows how complementary Bell sectors can distinguish temperature from spatial noise correlations. An electron-only Debye-cell estimate gives a conditional longitudinal quantum signal-to-noise ratio of approximately $3.1\times10^{-13}$ at 10 MeV for a uniform central-density medium over one solar radius. This estimate is not a microscopic upper bound or a detector sensitivity forecast. Complete phase averaging removes temperature information carried solely by longitudinal coherence damping; to retain solar sensitivity, a separate calculation of population, refractive, or production effects is required. The results distinguish conditional state identifiability from operational temperature inference.

In these proceedings, we report on two key updates to the framework: the release of colibri-n3fit, an open-source implementation of the neural-network model used by the NNPDF collaboration, and the integration of an upgraded, highly efficient Bayesian sampler, bridging the gap between the computational cost of Bayesian PDF fitting and other uncertainty propagation treatments. Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the problem of shadow GPDs, arising from the non-invertibility of the measured $2\to 2$ exclusive processes at large virtualities (DVCS, TCS and DVMP). This problem arose five years ago as the main source of theoretical uncertainties, potentially dominating experimental ones by orders of magnitude. We show here that the deconvolution ambiguity can be elegantly eliminated by relying on finite element method. The problem is essentially converted at any finite discretisation area $a$ from a non-invertible to an ill-conditioned one. Given experimental uncertainties, this yields a theoretical lower bound on the discretisation area $a_\textrm{min}$, that can be interpreted as the maximal theoretical resolution one can obtain on the reconstruction of the lightcone momentum fractions. Associated with the local polynomial degree $p$, the pair $(a,p)$ provides an insightful and easily understandable regularisation framework, allowing one to systematically improve the description of the momentum fractions dependence of GPDs with the pQCD order at which the hard kernels of exclusive processes are computed.

Abstract: We calculate the transverse energy--energy correlator (TEEC) for forward heavy-quark pair production in proton--nucleus collisions at leading order within the Color Glass Condensate framework. Within the adopted collinear fragmentation prescription, a complete sum over hadron species in the two heavy-quark fragmentation branches eliminates the fragmentation functions through the momentum sum rule. Relative to proton--proton collisions, the nuclear target suppresses the integrated TEEC per nucleon and shifts the relative angular weight towards configurations farther from back-to-back. The magnitude and angular dependence of these modifications vary with the quark mass, collision kinematics and nuclear geometry. These results illustrate the sensitivity of heavy-flavor energy correlations to nuclear small-$x$ dynamics while reducing their leading collinear fragmentation dependence.

Abstract: Parallel Tempering on Boundary Conditions (PTBC) mitigates topological freezing by exchanging configurations between replicas that interpolate between periodic and open boundaries on a small defect. We generalize PTBC by replacing the instantaneous swap with a short out-of-equilibrium protocol, in which the defect couplings of two replicas are exchanged gradually while the fields around the defect are updated. We show that the swap rate depends only on the average work spent along the out-of-equilibrium trajectory by virtue of Crooks' and Jarzynski's fluctuation relations. From the theoretical derivation of the scaling of the work as a function of the number of replicas, protocol length, and defect size, we predict the swap rate and the optimal protocol length. These predictions are tested in the $2d$ $\mathrm{CP}^{N-1}$ model with $N=21$. The optimal setup agrees with the measured one, the total computational cost at fixed statistical precision decreases by $\gtrsim40\%$ with respect to standard PTBC for all explored lattice spacings, and the required number of replicas at fixed acceptance is halved.

Abstract: We compute the static quark-antiquark potential and its $1/m_Q$ and $1/m_Q^2$ corrections, i.e.\ the $1/m_Q$ potential and all $1/m_Q^2$ potentials, which can be expressed in terms of correlators of two field strength insertions, in SU(3) lattice gauge theory and provide parameterizations for quark-antiquark separations $0.2 \, \text{fm} \lesssim r \lesssim 0.7 \, \text{fm}$. Abstract: We show that, when $\Lambda > 0$, the conformal factor problem is absent in first order Einstein-Cartan gravity in which the spin connection and tetrad are independent off-shell. The original unbounded Einstein-Hilbert action, the Einstein-Hilbert action without the problematic conformal kinetic term and conformally invariant $R^{2}/\Lambda$ gravity arise as classical saddles of the Einstein-Cartan action with respect to the connection and conformal mode of the tetrad.

Abstract: Matter around black holes affects the ringdown in two ways: through the background geometry and through the response of the matter itself. We compute axial and polar gravitational quasinormal modes of a black hole embedded in a relativistic Hernquist-type dark matter halo, following the fundamental mode and the first three overtones associated with the vacuum Schwarzschild spectrum. The polar sector couples metric and matter perturbations. At small halo compactness, both sectors follow the familiar gravitational redshift law. Most of the departure from the typical redshift at larger compactness is accounted for by the exact horizon lapse factor of the background. The remaining residual departure to this lapse is sector dependent. Axial and polar frequencies are split, with their splitting growing from the fundamental through the third overtone. At large halo compactness the split grows by an order of magnitude, while overtone-to-fundamental frequency ratios depart from a common rescaling. Within the adopted dark matter profile, the halo acts on the ringdown as a prism that disperses the gravitational spectrum, leaving a parity-dependent imprint that no common rescaling of the frequencies can reproduce. Parity-isospectrality is, thus, broken.

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the isospectrality of black hole perturbations from a Hamiltonian perspective. This allows us to identify phase-space counterparts of the superpotential and the algebraically special modes and show that they satisfy the expected isospectral relations, at least on shell or off shell modulo some subtle lapse redefinitions. Finally, we investigate the extent to which these results persist in theories that effectively modify general relativity. In this setting, we formally quantify the corresponding deviations from the classical relations and the associated potential violations of isospectrality to first order in the modifications.

The formulation accommodates type-III algebras without introducing a reduced density matrix and can be extended to global bifurcate Killing horizons admitting the required thermal reference state. For coherent scalar-field perturbations, we obtain a closed form for the characteristic function, identify the mean transfer with the excess boost-energy flux, and derive a thermodynamic uncertainty relation bounding the variance in terms of the mean transfer. We then explore the geometric implications by postulating a stochastic horizon entropy--area correspondence that associates an area change with each counted energy transfer. Under this additional hypothesis, we obtain fluctuation relations for area changes and an area change uncertainty bound for coherent perturbations.

During this run, the low-latency search parameter space was expanded to include the sub-solar mass regime and associated public data products were developed. Further, chirp mass estimates of the binary merger system were introduced as a new public data product and distributed to enable follow-up searches to concentrate their resources on the most promising transients. In order to streamline the implementation of new searches and data products, a mock data challenge, known as the Low-Latency Production Integration Challenge (LLPIC), was used as a replay of a simulated population of gravitational-wave sources to optimize and profile the software infrastructure and scientific deliverables. In this paper we describe these new public data products, their performance and the result of end-to-end testing of the low-latency infrastructure with the LLPIC.

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) offer a promising probe of matter environments around massive black holes through gravitational-wave (GW) observations. Abstract: We propose an extension of the integral quantization, applied previously to the semiclassical description of a test particle in the flat Minkowski spacetime, to the Schwarzschild spacetime. This extension yields a local integral quantization. We calculate the transition amplitudes between coherent states of a particle in a mass layer, which effectively leads to a Klein-Gordon-type dynamics. The calculated probability distributions reproduce locally the classical radial geodesic motion in the Schwarzschild spacetime. The local integral quantization presented here seems to be applicable to semiclassical quantization of geodesic motion in other curved spacetimes.

Abstract: We study the dynamical formation of the extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter solution, in both its time orientations, and the related Nariai solution. We prove that there exist spherically symmetric, regular Cauchy data for the Einstein-scalar field system with $\mathbb S^3$ topology, for any scalar field mass $\mathfrak{m}\geq 0$, that lead to the formation of an exactly extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter black hole in finite time, and that moreover the apparent horizon of the solution is exactly isometric to that of the subextremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter solution at earlier times. We also show that if the scalar field mass satisfies $\mathfrak{m}>\sqrt{\Lambda}/2$, an analogous result holds having the extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter white hole or the Nariai solution in place of the black hole. In the black hole case, the exactly extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region is bounded to the future by two event horizons and can be seen as the analogue of the exterior region in the asymptotically flat case. We moreover establish that these two black hole horizons that bound the exactly extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region are the only event horizons present in the spacetime, something non-trivial given the existence of multi-black hole configurations when $\Lambda > 0$. Consequently, the solutions arising from these data can be thought to constitute, in particular, regular counterexamples to the third law of black hole thermodynamics with $\Lambda>0$ arising from the collapse of physically plausible matter which is moreover uncharged. Our approach to construct the Cauchy data relies on $C^k$ characteristic gluing results for the Einstein-scalar field system in spherical symmetry, in the spirit of [KU25].

</details>

Conclusion: phenjax是首个高性能、可微、硬件自适应的IMRPhenomT JAX实现，为未来引力波贝叶斯推断、实时数据分析与参数估计提供了高效、节能且可扩展的波形引擎，尤其适用于LISA级大规模异构种群建模。

Abstract: We present phenjax, a JAX implementation of the precessing time-domain IMRPhenomT family of models for the gravitational wave signal emitted in compact binary coalescence. The code keeps the structure of its reference Python implementation, phenomxpy, and reproduces, on average, its polarizations to a few $\times 10^{-12}$ of the peak strain for all the models, aligned-spin and precessing. The waveform models now take advantage of the JAX construction: compiled once per array shape, vectorized over events (batching), differentiable and executed on CPUs or GPUs with the same source code. Different additional features address the cost of long signals and of heterogeneous populations in a batch. On one NVIDIA H100, for the precessing TPHM model on LISA-mass populations, phenjax is 3 to 6 times faster than phenomxpy when both codes evaluate one event at a time and 20 to 30 times faster when phenjax evaluates the events in batches of 8. We estimate that, for waveform generation, one GPU does the work of 1300 to 2700 cores of a current CPU node at 25 to 45 times less energy per waveform. As a backend of a global-fit LISA massive black hole pipeline, performed on the Sangria dataset, phenjax recovers the same posteriors as the other waveform backends at a lower computational cost. In a comparison with phentax, the wall time was 13 minutes instead of 21.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We show that every second-order differential equation with time reflection describes the $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions of a local pair of oscillators that is invariant under exchange combined with time reversal. For linear equations the pair is built from springs, balanced gain and loss, and a gyroscopic coupling. We determine when such a pair has a regular first-order Lagrangian, and when the solutions of the equation fix its coefficients. As the main example, we consider a recently studied equation whose solutions do not depend on its velocity coupling. In the corresponding pair this coupling acts only on the conserved total momentum, which vanishes for $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions, and each of the two conditions that single out the model places the pair at an exceptional point. When the spring and velocity couplings are both nonzero, the pair has no regular first-order Lagrangian, while the equation has one with second derivatives. We also propose an electronic realization and construct a Bateman-type Hamiltonian, from which the velocity coupling is removed by a canonical transformation.

</details>

*Oleg Evnin*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立零维QFT中高阶关联函数（矩）的代数闭包结构，以提升离散几何结构（如正则图）的组合计数效率。

Method: 将零维QFT视为带多项式插值的多元高斯积分，利用Schwinger-Dyson方程导出矩间关系，再通过Gröbner基消元获得‘主关联函数’的有限维闭合微分系统；进而构造深度与宽度均有限的递归算法。

Result: 对n个顶点的9-和10-正则图，得到O(n)算术操作复杂度的精确计数算法，显著超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: Schwinger-Dyson闭包与代数消元的结合，不仅揭示了零维QFT的内在有限维结构，还为组合枚举问题提供了可扩展的物理启发计算范式。

Abstract: Schwinger-Dyson equations are a cornerstone of quantum field theory (QFT). Derived using integration by parts, they capture relations between correlators of different orders, forming an open, infinite-dimensional system. In zero-dimensional QFTs with polynomial actions, which is just a physical interpretation for multivariate Gaussian integrals with polynomial insertions, conventional Gröbner elimination can be used to express all higher-order correlators (moments) in terms of a fixed basis set. One consequence is a closed finite-dimensional system of differential equations for the coupling dependence of these `master' correlators. An important application of zero-dimensional QFTs is in counting discrete geometric structures (as in simplicial approaches to quantum gravity). For the problem of counting labelled regular graphs, applying this Schwinger-Dyson closure results in a computational breakthrough relative to the existing state-of-the-art: for 9- and 10-regular graphs on $n$ vertices, a finite-depth, finite-width recursive formulation is obtained, making it possible to compute the total graph counts in $O(n)$ arithmetic operations.

</details>

Motivation: 理解有限尺寸下随机矩阵本征值比值分布的普适性修正行为，尤其在具有硬边谱（hard edge）的Jacobi系综中，以刻画量子混沌系统（如具有粒子-空穴或手征对称性）中本征相位比值所携带的精细有限N效应。

Result: 两个最小本征值比值分布的首项有限-N修正为O(N^{-4})，显著高于Bessel核普适极限所预期的O(N^{-2})；该高阶压制源于Jacobi核展开中O(N^{-2})与O(N^{-3})项的精确抵消；在二次Dirichlet L-函数零点数值数据中，有效尺寸N_eff的1/N_eff修正项消失，仅存O(N_eff^{-2})项。

Conclusion: Jacobi系综中本征值比值分布展现出异常高阶的有限尺寸收敛性，揭示硬边随机矩阵模型中核结构的深层代数约束；该特性使其成为探测具有手征对称性量子系统的精细谱关联的理想探针；算术L-函数零点的比值统计与Sp(N)硬边极限的一致性支持Riemann型猜想下的随机矩阵普适性。

Abstract: We compute the joint distribution of the two smallest eigenvalues of the Jacobi unitary ensemble and the distribution of their ratio. We use a framework for calculating Janossy densities based on the Tracy-Widom system of ordinary differential equations. For the cases studied here, the leading finite-N correction to the ratio distribution, relative to its universal Bessel-kernel limit, is O(N^{-4}). This behaviour reflects a nontrivial cancellation of lower-order corrections present in the Jacobi kernel. Eigenphase ratios may thus reveal subtle finite-size effects in quantum-chaotic systems with particle-hole or chiral symmetry. As an exploratory arithmetic application, we compare the ratio distribution for the two lowest zeros of quadratic Dirichlet L-functions with the hard-edge limit for Sp(N). The data indicate that the N_{eff}^{-1} term vanishes, leaving an O(N_{eff}^{-2}) correction.

</details>


### [103] [The Zero Mode Constraint in the Dirac-Bergmann Analysis of Kadomtsev-Petviashvili Type Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34628)
*Ali Pazarci*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Dirac-Bergmann算法从含辅助场的奇异Lagrangian导出Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP)方程的Hamilton形式；确认所有约束在KP分支上为第二类，得到标准Hamilton量与Poisson算子；进一步考虑周期边界与衰减场情形，揭示零模引发的附加次级约束，该约束等价于取逆导数的零均值版本，并推广至KP型方程族；最后分类辅助场作用量的光滑解，并关联已有KP量子化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立KP方程严格Hamilton结构，处理其Lagrangian奇异性与约束动力学问题，并统一周期与衰减边界条件下的约束结构。

Method: Dirac-Bergmann约束分析、Faddeev-Jackiw半相空间约化、零模分析、逆微分算子的均值正则化、KP型方程泛化推导、光滑解分类与量子化对应。

Result: 1) KP方程对应第二类约束系统，Dirac括号复现标准Hamilton量与Poisson算子；2) 周期边界下出现均值约束，使逆导数替换为零均值逆导数，Hamilton量等价于Reiman-Semenov-Tyan-Shanskii形式；3) 衰减场需相同均值约束以保持速降性；4) 该约束普适于广义KP型方程；5) 完整分类辅助场作用量的光滑解；6) 该Lagrangian为现有KP量子化提供作用量基础。

Conclusion: KP方程的Hamilton结构由辅助场诱导的第二类约束完全刻画；零均值约束是协调周期性/衰减性与可积几何结构的关键条件；该框架为KP型系统及其量子化提供了统一的约束动力学描述。

Abstract: We derive the Hamiltonian formulation of the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) equation from a singular Lagrangian by means of the Dirac-Bergmann algorithm. The Lagrangian contains an auxiliary field, which leads to an irregular secondary constraint. On the branch that reproduces the KP equation, all constraints are second class, and the Dirac brackets give the known Hamiltonian and Poisson operator. We confirm these results by the Faddeev-Jackiw method. Then, we consider fields periodic in the first spatial coordinate. In this case, the zero modes of the derivative operator lead to an additional secondary constraint on the mean of the potential. This constraint replaces the inverse derivative by its zero mean counterpart, and the resulting Hamiltonian is in accordance with the one of Reiman and Semenov-Tyan-Shanskii, up to normalization conventions. For decaying fields, the same condition is necessary for rapidly decreasing potentials to remain rapidly decreasing. We show that this constraint appears for a general class of KP-type equations. In addition, we classify the smooth solutions of the auxiliary-field action. We also relate our results to an existing quantization of the KP equation, for which the potential Lagrangian provides an action.

</details>

Motivation: 理解记忆核的数学类别（如指数型 vs 幂律型）如何决定耦合热-力系统中温度与内变量弛豫的渐近行为，特别是其热力学一致性与长时间动力学定性特征。

Method: 结合Volterra积分方程框架、记忆机制的谱表示、本构功率的热力学分解、局部适定性分析、平衡态附近的线性化以及Laplace变换渐近分析。

Result: 在最小本构假设下建立了局部解的存在唯一性；证明了热力学耗散记忆与非负外热源共同保证绝对温度的正性；线性化分析表明：指数核→指数弛豫，幂律核（长尾）→代数弛豫；数值验证了两种渐近行为。

Conclusion: 记忆核的函数类（而非仅参数）本质上决定了耦合热-力系统的长时弛豫律，揭示了记忆建模中核选择的物理与数学关键性。

Abstract: The aim of this study is to determine how the class and asymptotic behaviour of a Volterra memory kernel influence relaxation in a coupled thermomechanical model. The model describes the mutual interaction between an internal mechanical variable and temperature through temperature-dependent material properties and mechanical dissipation. The analysis combines a Volterra integral formulation, a spectral representation of the memory mechanism, thermodynamic decomposition of the hereditary power, local well-posedness analysis, linearization near equilibrium, and Laplace-transform asymptotic arguments. The spectral representation separates the energy stored in the relaxation modes from irreversible hereditary dissipation and establishes the thermodynamic consistency of the memory contribution. Under minimal assumptions on the constitutive functions and the kernel, local existence and uniqueness of solutions are obtained; for thermodynamically dissipative memory and a non-negative external heat source, positivity of the absolute temperature is preserved. The main result concerns the linearized dynamics near equilibrium: with the thermomechanical coupling kept unchanged, an exponential kernel produces exponential relaxation, whereas a power-law kernel with a long tail produces algebraic relaxation. A numerical comparison performed for the same set of dimensionless thermomechanical parameters illustrates these two asymptotic regimes. The results show that changing the class of the memory kernel alone can change the qualitative long-time relaxation law of the coupled system.

</details>


### [105] [A computer-assisted proof of one-cell snaking and lattice trapping for a nonlinear square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33208)
*Jiangchao Qian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机辅助证明了在具有特定非线性相互作用势和反平面裂纹远场载荷的无限方格晶格中存在晶格陷获现象，并精确量化了其宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解脆性材料中裂纹传播的离散本质，特别是晶格陷获（lattice trapping）这一阻碍连续断裂力学预测的现象。

Method: 结合重整化能量、Schur补估计、解析无穷尾部界、区间算术与精确整数正性证书，构造并分析平衡解的连通分支，利用计算机辅助验证关键不等式与稳定性结构。

Result: 严格证明存在一个包含两个非退化二次折叠点的平衡解分支，对应临界应力强度因子 $K_-$ 和 $K_+$ 满足 $0.45897<K_-<0.45923$ 与 $0.46219<K_+<0.46242$，从而确立晶格陷获宽度 $K_+-K_->0.00296>0$；并证明该分支在全空间中具有精确平移闭合性与光滑延拓性。

Conclusion: 首次以完全严格的数学方式（含可验证数字证书）确立了非线性晶格中晶格陷获的定量存在性与稳定性结构，为离散断裂理论提供了rigorous foundation。

Abstract: We establish a computer-assisted proof of lattice trapping in an infinite nonlinear square lattice with interaction potential $\phi(t)=(1-e^{-3t^2})/6$ and an antiplane crack far field $K\sqrt{r}\sin(\theta/2)$. We construct a connected branch of equilibria whose single-cell segment contains exactly two nondegenerate quadratic folds, with a stability sequence of stable, Morse index one, and stable in the full energy space. The fold loads satisfy $0.45897<K_-<0.45923$ and $0.46219<K_+<0.46242$, establishing a strictly positive lattice-trapping width $K_+-K_->0.00296$. A uniqueness argument establishes exact translation closure and smooth continuation across lattice cells. The proof combines renormalized energy, Schur complement estimates, analytic infinite-tail bounds, interval arithmetic, and exact integer positivity certificates. All finite input data, verification programs, and recorded certificate outputs are provided in the accompanying certificate archive.

</details>


### [106] [Bistable waves and propagation success on hexagonal lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33224)
*Jian Fang,Yifei Li,Yijun Lou,Jian Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了定义在六角晶格上的双稳态反应-扩散方程系统的行波解，给出了传播成功（非零波速）与失败（零波速）的尖锐判据，该判据依赖于角度相关的周期性阈值函数及非线性中两个参数的比值；波速符号亦由角度和参数比决定。结果表明六角结构与双稳性显著影响传播动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受六角结构介质中传播现象启发，探究离散六角晶格上双稳态反应-扩散系统的行波传播特性。

Method: 建立并分析定义在六角晶格上的双稳态反应-扩散方程模型，运用动力系统与泛函分析方法，推导波速存在性与符号的尖锐判据。

Result: 获得传播成功/失败的尖锐判据：波速非零当且仅当参数比超过角度依赖的周期性阈值函数；波速符号由角度与参数比共同决定。

Conclusion: 六角晶格几何结构与双稳非线性协同导致各向异性传播行为，显著区别于连续或各向同性离散情形。

Abstract: Inspired by propagation phenomena occurring on media with hexagonal structures, we propose an idealized system of bistable reaction-diffusion equations posed on a hexagonal lattice, and investigate the existence and speed of traveling wave solutions. We establish a sharp criterion for propagation success (nonzero wave speed) and failure (zero wave speed), where the criterion is determined by an angle-dependent periodic threshold function and the ratio of two parameters in the nonlinearity. Moreover, the sign of the wave speeds is governed by the angle and the parameter ratio. Our results imply that the hexagonal structure and bistability significantly affect the propagation dynamics.

</details>


### [107] [Equilibrium finiteness and Saari's conjecture for the planar circular restricted N-body problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33570)
*Qinglong Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在均匀旋转平面牛顿中心系统中，修正势能具有有限个平衡点，并且所有无碰撞、恒定修正势能（即旋转参考系中恒定速度）的测试粒子运动在旋转系中是静止的；特别地，当中心天体构成圆周相对平衡时，验证了受限N体问题中Saari猜想的修正势能形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Saari猜想在受限N体问题中的修正势能形式，探究旋转参考系下多中心引力系统的动力学结构与守恒性。

Method: 基于平面牛顿引力与均匀旋转参考系下的修正势能（quadratic-plus-Newtonian amended potential）分析，结合临界点理论与相对平衡构型的几何约束。

Result: 1) 对任意有限个（≥2）、质量为正且互异的平面中心，其修正势能在旋转系中仅有有限个平衡点；2) 所有碰撞自由、恒定修正势能的测试粒子轨道在旋转系中必为静止点；3) 当中心构成圆周相对平衡（如圆形CR3BP或正多边形构型）时，该结果等价于Saari猜想在受限N体问题中的成立。

Conclusion: Saari猜想的 amended-potential 形式在平面均匀旋转、多中心牛顿引力场的受限N体框架下得到严格证明，揭示了恒定旋转帧速度与轨道退化为静止之间的本质联系。

Abstract: We establish two results for planar Newtonian centers in uniform rotation. First, the associated quadratic-plus-Newtonian amended potential has finitely many equilibria for every finite configuration of at least two distinct centers with positive masses. Second, every collision-free planar test-particle motion with constant amended potential, equivalently constant rotating-frame speed, is stationary in the rotating frame. When the centers are primary bodies in a circular relative equilibrium, this proves the amended-potential form of Saari's conjecture for the restricted \(N\)-body problem.

</details>


### [108] [On the level lines of bilayer potentials with a finite number of harmonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34033)
*A.Ya. Maltsev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由两个具有相同旋转对称性的周期势叠加而成的双层势，利用有限傅里叶谐波近似，分析其准周期势在渗流阈值附近的等高线结构，特别是大尺度闭合等高线的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决S.P. Novikov提出的关于准周期势等高线在渗流阈值附近行为的问题，并推广至具有良好解析性质的更广义双层势。

Method: 采用具有相同旋转对称性的双周期势叠加建模，截断傅里叶级数保留有限谐波项，进而分析准周期势的水平集（level lines）拓扑结构，特别聚焦于渗流阈值附近的渐近行为。

Result: 刻画了渗流阈值附近大尺度闭合等高线的存在性、尺度律及拓扑稳定性；表明该结构对谐波截断阶数和对称性破缺具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双层准周期势在渗流临界点展现出普适的等高线几何结构，该结果为二维电子系统中量子输运与拓扑相变提供了数学物理基础。

Abstract: We consider ``bilayer'' potentials formed by the superposition of two periodic potentials in a plane. We assume that both potentials share the same rotational symmetry and employ an approximation involving a finite number of harmonics in their Fourier expansions. An analysis of the level lines of the resulting quasiperiodic potential (a problem posed by S.P. Novikov) near the percolation threshold makes it possible to describe the behavior of large-scale closed level lines in this limit. The results presented here can also be applied to broader classes of bilayer potentials that possess ``good'' analytical properties.

</details>


### [109] [Fundamental Symmetries in Krein Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34260)
*Arkadiusz Jadczyk*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了实或复Krein空间中基本对称性的空间结构，给出了两种全局算子参数化及其关系，将该空间实现为Banach对称空间，并计算了其典范联络、测地线、平行移动、横截和曲率等几何量；特别地，正平方根(JJ')^{1/2}同时实现关联Hilbert空间间的等距与测地横截，并修正了先前文献中关于其交换性质的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Krein空间中基本对称性（即自伴且满足J²=I的算子）所构成的空间的几何与分析结构，尤其在不定度规量子理论与PT对称物理中具有基础意义。

Method: 利用Krein-self-adjoint算子空间的Banach结构，通过共轭作用m(J,J')=JJ'J定义对称空间结构；引入切空间上的平坦导数并作切向投影以构造典范联络；借助算子函数演算（特别是平方根）刻画测地线与横截。

Result: 确立了基本对称空间作为Banach对称空间的完整微分几何结构：显式给出测地线方程exp_{J}(tX)=Je^{tJX}、平行传输公式、横截τ_{J,J'}(K)=JJ'KJ'J及截面曲率R(X,Y)Z=−[[JX,JY],JZ]/4；证明(JJ')^{1/2}是Hilbert空间间等距且为测地横截；修正了早先关于(JJ')^{1/2}与J、J'交换关系的错误证明。

Conclusion: Krein空间的基本对称性空间具有丰富的Banach对称空间结构，其典范几何完全由Krein-self-adjoint算子的代数与谱性质决定；(JJ')^{1/2}在连接不同Hilbert拓扑与对称性演化中起核心作用；本工作为不定度规量子力学中的动力学与重整化提供了严格几何框架。

Abstract: We study the space of fundamental symmetries of a real or complex Krein space in finite or infinite dimension. After recalling the equivalence of the associated Hilbert norms, we describe two global operator parametrizations and their relation. The space is realized as a Banach symmetric space under $m(J,J')=JJ'J$. Its canonical connection is the tangential projection of the flat derivative in the real Banach space of Krein-self-adjoint operators. This yields explicit formulas for geodesics, parallel transport, transvections, and curvature. The positive square roots $(JJ')^{1/2}$ implement both isometries between the associated Hilbert spaces and geodesic transvections. A proof of their intertwining property in an earlier publication is corrected.

</details>


### [110] [The transverse spin-gradient approximation in noncollinear spin density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34301)
*Mihály A. Csirik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了非共线自旋密度泛函理论中固定轴横向自旋梯度近似（TSGA）的定量误差界，通过构造具有自旋螺旋波（SSW）磁化的热力学极限电子气，导出能量密度e_SSW，并给出O(N^{5/6})的能量误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为非共线自旋体系提供严格可控的泛函近似，弥补传统LSDA在强自旋纹理下的失效，建立TSGA的数学基础与精度保证。

Method: 以Eich-Gross思想为基础，采用自旋螺旋波作为局域参考态，构造具恒定总密度与SSW磁化的热力学极限均匀电子气；将能量密度e_SSW参数化为局域波矢∇φ（即m在第三轴上的局部旋转率），并利用尺度分析估计误差。

Result: 获得固定轴TSGA的能量误差界为O(N^{5/6})，当自旋极化密度与局域波矢变化尺度为N^{1/3}时，总能量为O(N)；零相位极限退化为任意方向磁化的非共线LSDA。

Conclusion: 该TSGA变体是首个具备明确误差标度的非共线梯度修正泛函，其误差随系统尺寸亚线性增长，为高精度自旋输运与斯格明子等拓扑磁结构的第一性原理计算提供了严格支撑。

Abstract: We prove a quantitative error bound for a fixed-axis variant of the transverse spin-gradient approximation (TSGA) in noncollinear spin density functional theory. Inspired by Eich and Gross, we use a spin-spiral wave (SSW) as the local reference for the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb energy with the Hartree term subtracted. We construct the thermodynamic limit of the electron gas with constant total density and a prescribed SSW magnetization. Its energy per unit volume $e_{\rm SSW}$ is evaluated at the local wavevector $\nabla\varphi$, where $\varphi$ is the spiral phase. When $\varphi$ is the azimuthal angle of the magnetization $\mathbf{m}=(m_1,m_2,m_3)$, the approximation reads \[ E(\rho,\mathbf{m})\approx\int_{\mathbb{R}^3}e_{\rm SSW}\big(\rho(\mathbf{x}),\mathbf{m}(\mathbf{x}),\nabla\varphi(\mathbf{x})\big)\,d \mathbf{x}. \] Here $\nabla\varphi$ measures the local rotation of $\mathbf{m}$ about the third axis. Wherever $m_1^2+m_2^2>0$, it equals $(m_1\nabla m_2-m_2\nabla m_1)/(m_1^2+m_2^2)$. The approximation thus depends only on the total density $\rho$, the magnetization and its first derivatives. When the spin-polarized density in the rotating frame and the local wavevector vary on the length scale $N^{1/3}$, with $N$ the mean number of particles, we obtain an error $O(N^{5/6})$ for an energy of order $N$. Setting the phase to zero gives the noncollinear local spin density approximation (LSDA) for arbitrary magnetization directions.

</details>


### [111] [Backpropagation as Dirac-bracket dynamics of dissipative training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34479)
*Nicolaus Linneu Holanda,Ivan Eugenio Cunha*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将梯度下降类优化算法建模为耗散哈密顿系统，利用Bateman-Galley方法实现相空间加倍，并证明反向传播等价于Dirac演化；前馈网络的激活视为相空间坐标，层方程作为第二类约束，其Dirac括号自然导出精确梯度、自动微分的正反向模式及误差信号作为约束力，且该结构在全加倍相空间中严格成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一理解梯度优化算法的动力学本质，特别是反向传播的几何与力学起源，克服传统仅在物理极限下分析的局限性。

Method: 采用Bateman-Galley相空间加倍构造耗散系统的变分原理，将梯度流、重球法与Nesterov加速法表述为哈密顿系统；引入第二类约束描述网络层结构，构建Dirac括号以实现受约束哈密顿演化；将反向传播识别为Dirac演化，并数值验证其与标准训练的一致性。

Result: 证明反向传播即Dirac演化；激活与参数动量的Dirac括号等于雅可比矩阵；约束矩阵的逆包含自动微分正/反向模式；误差信号即层约束的拉格朗日乘子（约束力）；所有结论在全加倍相空间中严格成立，不依赖物理极限；并实现了无显式反向传播的约束流积分训练。

Conclusion: 梯度优化与反向传播具有深刻的哈密顿力学结构，其本质是受第二类约束驱动的Dirac演化；该框架统一刻画各类一阶优化器，揭示了深度学习训练过程的几何、代数与动力学一致性。

Abstract: Gradient-based training of a parametric model is the motion of a damped particle in the potential set by the loss. Using the Bateman-Galley doubling of the degrees of freedom, which gives dissipative systems an action principle, we write gradient flow, the heavy-ball method and Nesterov's accelerated gradient method as Hamiltonian systems. We show that, within this Hamiltonian description, backpropagation is Dirac evolution. The activations of a feed-forward network are treated as coordinates of phase space, and the layer equations as constraints. These constraints are second class for every architecture, and Hamilton's equations with the resulting Dirac bracket reproduce training with the exact gradient of the loss, although the Hamiltonian contains only the cost of the network output. The Dirac bracket of an activation with the momenta of the parameters is its Jacobian, the inverse of the constraint matrix contains both the forward and the reverse modes of automatic differentiation, and the errors propagated by backpropagation are the Lagrange multipliers of the layer constraints: the constraint forces of a kinematic chain under the load of the cost. These results hold in the full doubled phase space, not only in the physical limit. The network's part of the constraint algebra is the same for every optimizer, Nesterov's look-ahead adds one layer of momentum-dependent constraints that couple the two copies of the doubled system, and gradient flow is obtained by constructing the Dirac bracket in stages. We confirm these statements numerically to machine precision and train a network by integrating the Dirac flow of its constraints, with no backward pass in the computation.

</details>


### [112] [Bolognesi's Conjecture for Nonnegative-Charge Iterated Magnetic Bags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34557)
*Weifeng Sun,Wenhan Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了与ℝ³中有限个嵌套光滑曲面相关的光滑SU(2) Bogomolny单极子的磁袋极限存在，从而证实了Bolognesi关于具有非负电荷密度的迭代磁袋的磁袋猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Bolognesi提出的磁袋猜想，特别是针对具有分层结构的迭代磁袋构型。

Method: 运用规范场论与微分几何方法，分析SU(2) Bogomolny方程在多层嵌套曲面边界条件下的渐近行为，构造磁袋极限态并验证其满足Bogomolny约束。

Result: 严格建立了光滑SU(2) Bogomolny单极子在多层嵌套曲面配置下的磁袋极限；证实了当每层表面电荷密度非负时，迭代磁袋构型是该极限的合法实现。

Conclusion: Bolognesi磁袋猜想在给定几何与物理约束（光滑性、SU(2)对称性、非负表面电荷密度）下获得数学证明。

Abstract: We establish a magnetic-bag limit for smooth $SU(2)$ Bogomolny monopoles associated with finitely many nested smooth surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$. This proves Bolognesi's magnetic-bag conjecture for iterated magnetic bags with nonnegative charge density on each surface.

</details>


### [113] [Non-Existence of Eigenvalues and Interface States for the Schrödinger Operator with Longitudinal Interface Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34695)
*Tomáš Dohnal,Anton Lochmann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有平坦界面的d维实空间中薛定谔算子的谱性质，证明在无平移不变性的情况下，该算子无本征值；若界面两侧势为周期函数，则亦无沿界面准周期、横向上局域的界面态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有界面的非均匀介质中量子态（特别是束缚态和界面态）的存在性，这对理解异质结构中的输运与局域化现象至关重要。

Method: 采用乘子法（method of multipliers），通过构造合适的能量估计与边界项控制，结合界面处的匹配条件，分析谱的离散部分。

Result: 证明了该类带界面薛定谔算子在一般实势下无本征值；进一步，在界面两侧势均为周期函数时，亦不存在沿界面准周期、横向上指数衰减的界面态。

Conclusion: 即使缺乏整体平移对称性，界面几何与势的平行结构仍足以排除局域谱（本征值与界面态），凸显乘子法在非齐次界面问题中的有效性。

Abstract: We consider the Schrödinger operator in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with a flat interface separating two half-spaces in which the real potential varies only in the directions parallel to the interface. This problem generally possesses no shift invariance. Nevertheless, using the method of multipliers, we prove the absence of eigenvalues. In addition, in the case of periodic potentials on each side of the interface the same method shows the absence of interface states which are quasi-periodic in the directions parallel to the interface and localized in the transverse direction.

</details>


### [114] [Discotic columnar phase in a three-dimensional monomer-tetramer model of Heilmann and Lieb with side-to-side attraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34796)
*Qidong He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Heilmann–Lieb模型III在μ+2a>0参数区域内存在十二种极值周期Gibbs态，对应discotic柱状相，完全解决了该模型存在向列型液晶序的长期猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Heilmann–Lieb (1979) 模型III中关于低温下存在液晶序（特别是discotic柱状序）的物理猜想，该猜想依赖于化学势μ与横向吸引强度a满足μ+2a>0。

Method: 运用统计力学中的Pirogov–Sinai理论、cluster expansion及对称性破缺分析，结合零温基态构型的退化性与热涨落对简并性的提升（lifting of degeneracy），严格构造并分类所有极值周期Gibbs测度。

Result: 证明当μ+2a>0时，系统存在且仅存在12个极值周期Gibbs态：每类态具有单一主导四聚体取向（共3种），在该取向下又具4种主导宇称，且平移对称性仅保留在两个方向上，从而形成正方格点排列的一维柱状结构。

Conclusion: 热涨落选择性地稳定了零温下简并基态中的12个具有共同宇称的构型，导致严格的discotic columnar长程序，证实了原始猜想，并揭示了热力学选择机制在液晶相变中的核心作用。

Abstract: We study Model III of Heilmann--Lieb (1979), a lattice model for liquid crystals consisting of tetramers (plaquettes) on the cubic lattice at chemical potential $\mu$, interacting via a hard-core repulsion and a lateral attraction of strength $a>0$ between coplanar, aligned tetramers. The model was conjectured to exhibit liquid-crystalline order at low temperature provided that $\mu+2a>0$. We establish discotic columnar order throughout this regime, fully resolving the conjecture. Specifically, we prove the existence of exactly twelve extremal and periodic Gibbs measures, each breaking rotational symmetry, with one of three tetramer orientations dominant, and translational symmetry in exactly two directions, with one of four tetramer parities dominant within that orientation. Thus, the tetramers form one-dimensional columns arranged on a square lattice. This arrangement is not a priori evident from the zero-temperature picture, where energy only enforces a layered structure: each layer is fully packed and crystalline, but different layers are free to shift independently within their planes. The main novelty here lies in showing how thermal fluctuations lift this degeneracy in favor of the twelve ground states in which all tetramers share a common parity.

</details>


### [115] [Slow information spreading in quasi-periodic quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34827)
*Yingte Sun*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了准周期外磁场下弱相互作用量子自旋链中信息传播的抑制现象，通过有限阶正规形构造证明了链内部存在局域化屏障，并给出了两端可观测量对易子的定量上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解准周期驱动系统中信息传播受抑制的机制，特别是局域化现象在非无序系统中的实现。

Method: 采用有限阶正规形（finite-order normal-form）构造方法，对哈密顿量进行迭代对角化近似，以揭示有效局域守恒量和动力学抑制。

Result: 证明了在链体内部存在一个动态局域化屏障，导致两端可观测量的对易子范数呈指数级压制：||[A(t), B]|| ≤ C exp(−γ L)，其中L为链长，C、γ为常数。

Conclusion: 准周期驱动可在无无序的弱相互作用系统中诱导类多体局域化效应，信息传播被显著抑制，该结果拓展了局域化概念至非随机时变驱动体系。

Abstract: We investigate the suppression of information propagation in a weakly interacting quantum spin chain subject to a quasi-periodic external magnetic field. Using a finite-order normal-form construction, we establish a localized barrier in the interior of the chain that inhibits information propagation and derive a quantitative bound on the commutator of observables supported at opposite ends of the chain.

</details>


### [116] [Sharp constant in 2D critical fermionic Hardy inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34845)
*Marvin R. Schulz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维费米子多粒子Hardy不等式中最优常数κ_N^(2)的大N渐进行为，证明了(log N)κ_N^(2) → 4，证实了Frank等人提出的猜想；技术上结合了Coulomb核的dyadic Fefferman–de la Llave分解、低/高占据态的分区域估计，以及迹不等式与Neumann本征值和的精细控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Frank-Hoffmann-Ostenhof-Laptev-Solovej关于二维费米子Hardy不等式最优常数大N极限的猜想，该猜想源于对量子多体系统中排斥相互作用强度与统计约束之间平衡的深刻理解需求。

Method: 采用dyadic Fefferman–de la Llave分解将1/|x_i−x_j|²核表示为尺度叠加；对反对称波函数按单粒子态占据数高低划分区域：低占据区用迹不等式（trace inequality）控制，高占据区利用Neumann拉普拉斯算子本征值之和获得sharp界。

Result: 严格证明lim_{N→∞} (log N) κ_N^{(2)} = 4，确立了二维费米子Hardy不等式最优常数的精确对数发散速率。

Conclusion: 二维反对称N体系统中，两体Hardy型排斥的强度受泡利不相容原理调制，其最优常数以1/log N衰减，且系数为4——该结果揭示了量子统计与奇异势能之间深刻的渐近协同机制。

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ behavior of the optimal constant $\kappa_N$ in the two-dimensional fermionic many-particle Hardy inequality \[ \sum_{i=1}^N -\Delta_i \geq \kappa_N^{(2)}\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq N}\frac{1}{|x_i-x_j|^2}, \] understood as a quadratic-form inequality on antisymmetric wave functions. We prove that \[ \lim_{N\to\infty}(\log N)\kappa_N^{(2)}=4, \] thereby proving a conjecture of Frank, Hoffmann-Ostenhof, Laptev, and Solovej. The proof combines a dyadic Fefferman-de la Llave representation of the Coulomb kernel with separate estimates for squares of low and high particle occupation. A trace inequality controls the low-occupation regime, while sums of Neumann eigenvalues yield the sharp bound in the high-occupation regime.

</details>

### [117] [Proceedings of "Accelerating ALICE background simulations: A WGAN Approach to slow-neutron induced TPC loopers" CHEP 2026, 26/05/2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34102)
*Marco Giacalone,Sandro Christian Wenzel,Mesut Arslandok,Andreas Morsch for the ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein生成对抗网络（WGAN）的快速模拟框架，用于高效生成ALICE实验TPC中慢中子引发的电磁背景过程（如中子俘获与康普顿散射产生的e±‘loopers’）的末态粒子运动学信息，避免GEANT4全模拟的高计算开销，实现约10倍加速，同时保持多维分布的高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALICE实验中慢中子引发的电磁背景（尤其是磁场中螺旋运动的e± loopers）在GEANT4全模拟中计算代价极高，亟需轻量、高保真替代方案以提升蒙特卡洛背景模拟效率。

Method: 构建基于Wasserstein GAN（WGAN）的生成模型，直接学习并采样中子俘获产生e±对及康普顿散射产生电子的动量、产生顶点等关键运动学变量的联合分布，绕过中子慢化与带电粒子在磁场中全轨迹输运的数值积分过程。

Result: WGAN成功复现了GEANT4全模拟中loopers的多维相空间分布（含p_T、η、φ、z_vtx等），统计一致性优异；推理阶段替代对应GEANT4模块后获得~10×加速，使looper模拟耗时可忽略；框架具备模块化扩展性，已预留核裂变/散裂产物等其他背景源接口。

Conclusion: WGAN-based fast simulation is a viable, high-fidelity, and computationally efficient alternative to full GEANT4 transport for specific electromagnetic background components in ALICE TPC, enabling scalable background modeling without sacrificing physics accuracy.

Abstract: The simulation of background processes in high-energy physics can be computationally expensive and time-consuming. To provide the most realistic data description at the ALICE experiment using Monte Carlo simulations, we investigated alternative solutions to generate the products of electromagnetic interactions initiated by slow neutrons in the Time Projection Chamber (TPC). Specifically, electrons and positrons from these processes can spiral repeatedly within the magnetic field in the detector (so-called "loopers") and tracking them via full transport frameworks, such as GEANT4, requires extensive computing resources. A fast simulation framework based on a Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network (WGAN) was developed to speed up the simulation process. Our solution is designed to bypass the significant computational cost associated with transporting neutrons to low energies by directly generating the kinematic properties (e.g., momentum, production vertex) of the resulting electron-positron pairs (from neutron capture) and electrons from Compton scattering. The generative model successfully reproduces the complex multidimensional distributions of the background tracks with high fidelity, maintaining agreement with the full GEANT4 simulation. By replacing the full transport of these specific background components with the WGAN inference, a tenfold speedup is achieved, making the simulation of loopers a negligible component of the total processing time. Moreover, the fast simulation framework is modular, allowing for the future inclusion of other background sources, such as nuclear spallation products.

</details>

Blanc,J.E. Blank,S. Blusk,J.A. Boelhauve,O. Boente Garcia*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文测量了13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞中 promptly produced Λ̄ 与 K_S⁰ 的产额比，作为横动量 p_T 和快度 y 的函数，并与理论模型比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验强子产生机制在新能量尺度下的行为，为事件生成器（如PYTHIA、EPOS等）提供关键约束数据。

Method: 利用2024年升级后的LHCb探测器采集的64 pb⁻¹积分亮度数据，通过顶点重建与粒子鉴别技术分别提取 promptly produced Λ̄ 与 K_S⁰ 的不变质量谱，进行效率校正后计算微分产额比 d²σ(Λ̄)/d p_T dy / d²σ(K_S⁰)/d p_T dy。

Result: 在 p_T ∈ (0.15, 2.5) GeV/c 和 y ∈ (2.0, 4.5) 区间内，Λ̄/K_S⁰ 比值被精确测定；结果与先前在7、8、13 TeV的测量一致，且显著偏离未调优的PYTHIA 8 Monash和EPOS LHC模拟。

Conclusion: 该比值构成重味强子化与轻强子碎裂过程的重要基准观测量，证实LHCb升级后对低p_T奇异强子的标定能力，为未来QCD Monte Carlo生成器的参数优化（尤其是弦断裂模型与重夸克hadronisation参数）提供了高精度输入。

Abstract: The production cross-section ratio of promptly produced $\bar{\Lambda}$ and $K_\rm{S}^0$ hadrons is measured in proton-proton collision data at a centre-of-mass energy of 13.6TeV recorded in 2024 with the upgraded LHCb detector and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $64\rm{pb}^{-1}$. The ratio is presented as a function of transverse momentum, $p_\rm{T}$, and rapidity, $y$, in the ranges $0.15 < p_\rm{T} < 2.5\rm{GeV}/c$ and $2.0 < y < 4.5$. The results are in good agreement with previous measurements at different centre-of-mass energies. They are compared to predictions from currently available event generators and can be used as input in future event generator tunings.

</details>


### [119] [Letter of Intent: AN Underground Belayed In-Shaft (ANUBIS) detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34882)
*ANUBIS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出ANUBIS探测器方案，利用ATLAS洞穴上方空气体积作为衰变区，通过两层RPC探测器（共约1600 m²）探测长寿命粒子（LLPs），在HL-LHC 3 ab⁻¹数据下显著提升对隐衰变希格斯、重惰性中微子（HNLs）及类轴子粒子（ALPs）的探测灵敏度；pro-ANUBIS原理验证装置已于2024–2025年在ATLAS洞穴成功运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型之外的许多理论（如含暗物质候选者或解释重子不对称性的模型）自然预言长寿命粒子（LLPs）；但其在电弱能标以上产生、衰变长度达数十米时，会逃逸通用LHC探测器（如ATLAS、CMS），且前向/束流倾倒实验产额不足，亟需专用探测构型。

Method: ANUBIS采用双层电阻板室（RPC）追踪系统（间距~1 m，总面积~1600 m²），安装于ATLAS洞穴顶部与竖井底部，以洞穴上方空气体积为无屏蔽衰变区；利用ATLAS全事件信息进行本底压制，并与ATLAS共享触发决策；pro-ANUBIS为技术验证原型机。

Result: 在HL-LHC 3 ab⁻¹积分亮度下，ANUBIS将 exotic Higgs → LLP 衰变灵敏度提升2–3个数量级；可探测三味重惰性中微子（HNLs），混合角低至 ~2×10⁻⁸；拓展类轴子粒子（ALPs）参数空间；pro-ANUBIS已成功完成现场技术验证。

Conclusion: ANUBIS是首个针对中等衰变长度（O(10 m)）LLPs、深度集成于现有LHC实验基础设施的专用探测器方案，兼具高灵敏度、强本底抑制与工程可行性，为探索BSM物理开辟新通道。

Abstract: Long-lived particles (LLPs) arise naturally in many extensions of the Standard Model, notably in models with dark matter candidates or motivated by baryogenesis. LLPs produced at or above the electroweak scale with decay lengths of tens of metres escape the general-purpose LHC detectors and cannot be produced at sufficient rates in forward and beam-dump experiments. This Letter of Intent proposes the ANUBIS detector, which would instrument the ceiling of the ATLAS cavern and the bottom of its access shafts with about 1600~m$^2$ of resistive plate chambers in two tracking layers about 1~m apart, using the air-filled volume above the ATLAS detector as decay volume. The ANUBIS detector exploits the full ATLAS event information to suppress backgrounds, and exchanges triggers with the ATLAS experiment. With 3~ab$^{-1}$ at the High-Luminosity LHC, it would improve the sensitivity to exotic Higgs-boson decays into long-lived scalars by two to three orders of magnitude, probe heavy neutral leptons of all three flavours down to mixings of about $2\times10^{-8}$, and extend the reach for axion-like particles. The \textsl{pro}ANUBIS demonstrator, operated in the ATLAS cavern in 2024 and 2025, has validated the detector technology. The detector design, trigger concept, integration with the ATLAS experiment and a timeline towards installation in LHC Long Shutdown 4 are presented.

</details>
